news_event = { #1 - Inauguration of Tjarda van Starkenborgh
	id = news_indonesia.1
	title = news_indonesia.1.t
	desc = news_indonesia.1.d
	picture = GFX_event_ins_pen_and_paper
	
	major = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.1.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				tag = INS
				tag = HOL
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.1.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					tag = INS
					tag = HOL
				}
			}
			OR = {
				has_country_leader_ideology = moderatism
				has_country_leader_ideology = centrism
				has_country_leader_ideology = despotism
				has_country_leader_ideology = conservatism
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.1.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					tag = INS
					tag = HOL
					has_country_leader_ideology = moderatism
					has_country_leader_ideology = centrism
					has_country_leader_ideology = despotism
					has_country_leader_ideology = conservatism
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

news_event = { #3 - Fall of Batavia
	id = news_indonesia.3
	title = news_indonesia.3.t
	desc = news_indonesia.3.d
	picture = GFX_event_ins_japan_batavia
	
	major = yes
	
	trigger = {
		1058 = { is_owned_by = INS }
		1058 = { is_controlled_by = JAP }
		JAP = {
			has_war_with = INS
		}
		NOT = { has_global_flag = fall_of_batavia_ins }
	}
	
	immediate = {
		set_global_flag = fall_of_batavia_ins
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 2
	}

	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.3.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				TAG = INS
				is_in_faction_with = INS
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.3.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				TAG = JAP
				is_in_faction_with = JAP
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = news_indonesia.3.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				TAG = INS
				TAG = JAP
				is_in_faction_with = INS
				is_in_faction_with = JAP
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #1 - De Jonge 61st birthday
	id = indonesia.1
	title = indonesia.1.t
	desc = indonesia.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_de_jonge_aging

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		has_government = neutrality
		date > 1936.01.02
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		NOT = { has_country_flag = INS_de_jonge_resigned }
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }
	
	option = {
		name = indonesia.1.a
		set_country_flag = INS_needs_governor
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Together for Victory"
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = indonesia_locks_discourage_tt
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indonesia.1.b
		HOL = {
			HOL_johannes_theodorus_furstner = {
				set_nationality = INS
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = INS_needs_governor
		set_country_flag = INS_furstner_stay_flag
		custom_effect_tooltip = indonesia_open_discourage_tt
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #2 - "There so much good to be done more" book release 
	id = indonesia.2
	title = indonesia.2.t
	desc = indonesia.2.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_helsdingen_book

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		date > 1936.02.20
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }

	option = { #good book
		name = indonesia.2.a
		add_political_power = 5
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}

	option = { #endorse it
		name = indonesia.2.b
		add_political_power = 10
		add_war_support = 0.02
		add_stability = -0.01
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

country_event = { #3 - Van Mook and co attends the expo
	id = indonesia.3
	title = indonesia.3.t
	desc = indonesia.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_van_mook_flight

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		has_government = neutrality
		date > 1936.02.03
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		USA = {
			exists = yes
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = INS
			}
		}
	}

    option = { #safe trip
		name = indonesia.3.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.4 days = 150 }
				}
				33 = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.5 days = 150 }
				}
				33 = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.6 days = 150 }
				}
			}
		}
    }
}

country_event = { #8 - Raad van State choose de Jonge's successor
	id = indonesia.8
	title = indonesia.8.t

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.8.d_1
		trigger = {
			INS = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = INS_furstner_stay_flag }
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.8.d_2
		trigger = {
			INS = {
				has_country_flag = INS_furstner_stay_flag
			}
		}
	}

	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_de_jonge_aging

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = indonesia.8.a #A.W.L. Tjarda
		set_country_flag = HOL_choose_tjarda_flag
		INS = { country_event = { id = indonesia.9 days = 56 } }

		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				INS = {
					focus_progress = {
						focus = INS_r56_discourage_democratic_reforms
						progress > 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indonesia.8.b #Furstner
		set_country_flag = HOL_choose_furstner_flag
		INS = { country_event = { id = indonesia.9 days = 56 } }

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				INS = {
					focus_progress = {
						focus = INS_r56_discourage_democratic_reforms
						progress > 0
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #9 - Dutch Raad choose Tjarda
	id = indonesia.9
	title = indonesia.9.t
	desc = indonesia.9.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_tjarda_jonge_handover

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #Hurrah
		name = indonesia.9.a
		set_country_flag = INS_de_jonge_resigned
		promote_character = INS_a_t_van_starkenborgh_stachouwer
		retire_character = INS_bonifacius_de_jonge
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			long_name = INS_neutrality_party_volksraad_long
			name = INS_neutrality_party_volksraad
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = democratic
			long_name = INS_democratic_party_volksraad_long
			name = INS_democratic_party_volksraad
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = { 
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						has_capitulated = no
						OR = {
							is_neighbor_of = HOL
							is_neighbor_of = INS
							tag = JAP
							is_subject_of = HOL
							is_subject_of = ENG
							is_subject_of = FRA
							is_subject_of = BEL
							is_southwest_pacific_state = yes
							num_subjects > 0
						}
					}
					news_event = {
						hours = 12
						id = news_indonesia.1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #90 - Dutch Raad choose Furstner
	id = indonesia.90
	title = indonesia.90.t
	desc = indonesia.90.d

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #Hurrah
		name = indonesia.90.a
		set_country_flag = INS_de_jonge_resigned
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = HOL_johannes_theodorus_furstner
			country_leader = {
				desc = HOL_johannes_furstner_govgen_desc
				expire = "1970.9.15"
				ideology = despotism
				traits = {
					strongman_indies
				}
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		retire_character = INS_bonifacius_de_jonge
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			long_name = INS_neutrality_party_volksraad_long
			name = INS_neutrality_party_volksraad
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = democratic
			long_name = INS_democratic_party_volksraad_long
			name = INS_democratic_party_volksraad
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = { 
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						has_capitulated = no
						OR = {
							is_neighbor_of = HOL
							is_neighbor_of = INS
							tag = JAP
							is_subject_of = HOL
							is_subject_of = ENG
							is_subject_of = FRA
							is_subject_of = BEL
							is_southwest_pacific_state = yes
							num_subjects > 0
						}
					}
					news_event = {
						hours = 12
						id = news_indonesia.11
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #11 - Hamengkubuwono Eighth's passing
	id = indonesia.11
	title = indonesia.11.t
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_hamengkubuwono_ninth_coronation

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.11.d_1
		trigger = {
			has_idea = INS_hubertus_van_mook
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.11.d_2
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_idea = INS_hubertus_van_mook }
				NOT = {
					has_country_leader = {
						name = "Sukarno"
						ruling_only = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.11.d_3
		trigger = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Sukarno"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
	}

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		date > 1939.10.22
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				has_government = fascism
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 100 }

	option = {
		name = indonesia.11.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_hamengkubuwono_eighth
			}
			set_country_flag = INS_hamengkubuwono_invited_flag
			remove_ideas = INS_hamengkubuwono_eighth
			add_ideas = INS_hamengkubuwono_ninth
		}
		retire_character = INS_hamengkubuwono_eighth
		set_country_flag = INS_hamengkubuwono_viii_passed
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = INS_r56_reestablish_royal_houses
			}
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = INS_hamengkubuwono_ninth
				traits = { INS_loyalty_to_indonesia politically_connected }
				skill = 1
				attack_skill = 1
				defense_skill = 1
				planning_skill = 1
				logistics_skill = 1
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			1086 = {
				add_manpower = -1
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #12 - INS new capital
	id = indonesia.12
	title = indonesia.12.t
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_batavia_parade

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.12.d_1
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = INS_r56_continue_reform
				AND = {
					has_completed_focus = INS_r56_discourage_democratic_reforms
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = INS_r56_revive_spirit_1926 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.12.d_2
		trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = INS_r56_revive_spirit_1926
			NOT = {
				has_country_leader = {
					character = INS_sukarno
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.12.d_3
		trigger = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = INS_sukarno
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #keep Batavia
		name = indonesia.12.a
		set_country_flag = INS_renew_batavia
		hidden_effect = {
			add_ideas = INS_build_capital_1
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				add = 40
				industrial_complex < 12
			}
		}

country_event = { #16 - Hart Retires
	id = indonesia.16
	title = indonesia.16.t
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_hart

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.16.d_1
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_completed_focus = INS_r56_reinstate_stuwgroep
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.16.d_2
		trigger = {
			has_completed_focus = INS_r56_reinstate_stuwgroep
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		has_character = INS_george_hart
		OR = {
			date > 1943.08.08
			check_variable = { var = INS_JAP_trade value = 2 compare = greater_than }
			has_completed_focus = INS_r56_emerald_equator
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }

	option = { #Accepted (van Mook)
		name = indonesia.16.a

		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = INS_r56_reinstate_stuwgroep }
		}

		set_country_flag = INS_hart_retired
		set_country_flag = INS_van_mook_coi
		hidden_effect = {
			INS_hubertus_van_mook = {
				add_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = coi_mook
				}
				remove_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = skilled_negotiator
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_george_hart
			}
			deactivate_advisor = INS_george_hart
			activate_advisor = INS_hubertus_van_mook
		}
		retire_character = INS_george_hart
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = { #Accepted (Hoogstraten)
		name = indonesia.16.b

		trigger = {
			 has_completed_focus = INS_r56_reinstate_stuwgroep
		}

		add_stability = 0.05
		add_political_power = 25

		set_country_flag = INS_hart_retired
		set_country_flag = INS_hoogstraten_coi

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_george_hart
			}
			deactivate_advisor = INS_george_hart
			activate_advisor = INS_hoogstraten
		}
		retire_character = INS_george_hart

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}

	option = { #pls work more for a bit
		name = indonesia.16.c

		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = INS_hart_overworked_flag
			}
		}
		
		add_political_power = -50
		add_stability = -0.05
		INS_george_hart = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = captain_of_industry
					add = coi_hart
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = coi_hart
				}
				remove_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = captain_of_industry
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_hart_overworked_flag
			country_event = { id = indonesia.16 days = 630 random_days = 100 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		any_country = {

					original_tag = JAP
					is_in_faction_with = JAP
				}
			}
			has_capitulated = no
		}
	}

		add_war_support = -0.075

			clr_country_flag = JAP_declare_war_indies_banned
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_literally_china = yes
				has_war_with = JAP
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = medium_increase
			}
		}
		HOL = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = small_decrease
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					original_tag = JAP
					is_in_faction_with = JAP
				}
				has_war_with = CHI
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = large_decrease
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_1
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_japanese_investment_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_2
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_japanese_investment_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_3

		}

		}
	}

		add_war_support = 0.05
		set_country_flag = INS_neutral_china_war_flag

		}

country_event = { #54 - The Queen returns home
	id = indonesia.54
	title = indonesia.54.t
	desc = indonesia.54.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_hol_wilhelmina_speech

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_global_flag = {
			flag = INS_wilhelmina_tropical_holiday_flag
			days > 180
		}
		NOT = {
			HOL = {
				has_war = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = indonesia.54.a #Long Live the Queen!!!
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = INS_indies_dominion_act
			days = 3650
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				var = hol_puppet_neutrality_var
				value = party_popularity@neutrality
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = HOL
					has_autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				}
				HOL = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = INS
						autonomous_state = autonomy_colony
					}
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
				set_political_party = {
					ideology = democratic
					popularity = 100
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = hol_puppet_neutrality_var
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = HOL
					has_autonomy_state = autonomy_integrated_puppet
				}
				HOL = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = INS
						autonomous_state = autonomy_colony
					}
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
				set_political_party = {
					ideology = democratic
					popularity = 100
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = hol_puppet_neutrality_var
				}
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = INS_wilhelmina_tropical_holiday_flag
		hidden_effect = {
			INS = {
				HOL_wilhelmina = {
					set_nationality = HOL
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #55 - Fall of the Fatherland
	id = indonesia.55
	title = indonesia.55.t

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.55.d_1
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_idea = INS_wilhelmina_here }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = indonesia.55.d_2
		trigger = {
			has_idea = INS_wilhelmina_here
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		HOL = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_government = communism }
					has_capitulated = yes
				}
				exists = no
				has_government = communism
			}
		}
		has_completed_focus = INS_r56_invite_queen_wilhelmina
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }

	immediate = {
		set_country_flag = INS_fall_of_netherlands_flag
	}
	
	option = { #We stand for the Fatherland
		name = indonesia.55.a
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = INS_staat_van_oorlog_en_van_beleg_idea
			days = 730
		}
	}

	option = { #Long live the Queen!
		name = indonesia.55.b
		trigger = {
			has_idea = INS_wilhelmina_here
		}
		remove_ideas = INS_wilhelmina_here
		promote_character = HOL_wilhelmina
		HOL = {
			HOL_juliana = {
				set_nationality = INS
			}
			HOL_prince_bernhard = {
				set_nationality = INS
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = INS_royal_family_indies_flag
		add_ideas = INS_staat_van_oorlog_en_van_beleg_idea
	}
}

country_event = { #56 - The Queen returns home, after the war
	id = indonesia.56
	title = indonesia.56.t
	desc = indonesia.56.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_hol_wilhelmina_speech

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		has_government = neutrality
		has_global_flag = {
			flag = INS_wilhelmina_tropical_holiday_flag
			days > 180
		}
		has_country_flag = INS_fall_of_netherlands_flag
		has_war = no
		is_subject_of = HOL
		HOL = {
			has_war = no
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }
	
	option = {
		name = indonesia.56.a
		clr_global_flag = INS_wilhelmina_tropical_holiday_flag
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = INS_indies_dominion_act
			days = 1825
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				var = hol_puppet_neutrality_var
				value = party_popularity@neutrality
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_dlc = "Together for Victory"
					is_subject_of = HOL
				}
				HOL = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = INS
						autonomous_state = autonomy_dominion
					}
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = HOL_wilhelmina_absent
						add_idea = HOL_wilhelmina_idea
					}
				}
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
				set_political_party = {
					ideology = democratic
					popularity = 100
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = hol_puppet_neutrality_var
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_tjarda_van_starkenborgh
			}
			promote_character = INS_a_t_van_starkenborgh_stachouwer
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_hubertus_van_mook
				has_country_flag = INS_ltgg_van_mook_flag
			}
			promote_character = INS_hubertus_van_mook
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = HOL_wilhelmina
			}
			remove_ideas = HOL_wilhelmina
			add_ideas = HOL_juliana
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			INS = {
				HOL_wilhelmina = {
					set_nationality = HOL
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		name = indonesia.759.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 66
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_opinion = {
					target = INS
					value > 24
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_full_control_of_state = 979
			}
			PNG = {
				transfer_state = 979
				set_country_flag = INS_marked_for_asian_league_flag
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = democratic
				}
				set_autonomy = {
					target = PNG
					autonomy_state = autonomy_supervised_state
				}
				else = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = PNG
						autonomy_state = autonomy_dominion

country_event = { #290 - Hidden effect to setup the historical Japanese occupation
	id = indonesia.101
	hidden = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = INS
		has_war_with = JAP
		1058 = { is_controlled_by = JAP }
		is_in_faction_with = HOL
		HOL = { has_war_with = JAP }
		surrender_progress > 0.7
		JAP = { #only fascism or neutrality
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Hirohito"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		has_civil_war = no
		#Don't fire if Japan is almost capitulated
		NOT = {
			any_state = {
				is_on_continent = asia
				NOT = { state = 282 }
				NOT = { state = 528 }
				NOT = { state = 529 }
				NOT = { state = 531 }
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_owned_by = JAP
			}
		}		

		#Don't fire if Surabaya, Medan, Manado, and Balikpapan are still secured
		NOT = {
			any_country = {
				is_ai = no
				NOT = { tag = JAP }
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = JAP }
				has_war_with = ROOT
				any_state = {
					is_on_continent = asia
					NOT = { state = 334 } #Balikpapan
					NOT = { state = 1049 } #Medan
					NOT = { state = 1051 } #Surabaya
					NOT = { state = 1056 } #Manado
					is_owned_by = ROOT
					is_controlled_by = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 2 }
	immediate = {
		country_event = {
			id = indonesia.102
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Japindo 6 - Japan declares war on America
	id = indonesia.105
	title = indonesia.105.t
	desc = indonesia.105.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_usa_pearl_harbor_airphoto

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = JAP
		}
		OR = {
			is_puppet_of = HOL
			is_in_faction_with = HOL
		}
		HOL = {
			OR = {
				has_government = democratic
				has_government = neutrality
			}
		}
		USA = {
			has_government = democratic
			has_defensive_war_with = JAP
		}
	}

	option = { # No
		name = indonesia.105.a
		add_autonomy_ratio = {
			value = -0.5
		}
		add_stability = -0.15
		add_political_power = -100
	}

	option = { #Stand by them
		name = indonesia.105.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_1
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_japanese_investment_1
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = INS_japanese_surprise_1
				days = 365
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_2
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_japanese_investment_2
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = INS_japanese_surprise_2
				days = 183
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_japanese_investment_3
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_japanese_investment_3
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = INS_japanese_surprise_3
				days = 91
			}
		}
		INS = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = USA enemy = JAP hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
	}
}

				75 = { } # Nothing happens 
				# the "Western" Front
				15 = { # Aceh uprising
					modifier = {
						add = INS_civil_war_weeks
						always = yes
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 1.5 # To keep Aceh uprising always slightly more likely than the others
						always = yes
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = { 
							has_country_flag = ac_uprising_flag
							ATJ = { exists = yes }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { INS_nationalist_country = ATJ }
					set_country_flag = atj_uprising_flag
					INS = { country_event = indonesia.309 }
				}
				# the "Eastern" Front
				10 = { # Molukker uprising
					modifier = {
						add = INS_civil_war_weeks
						always = yes
					}
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = { 
							has_country_flag = mlk_uprising_flag
							MLK = { exists = yes }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { INS_nationalist_country = MLK }
					set_country_flag = mlk_uprising_flag
					INS = { country_event = indonesia.309 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	desc = indonesia.415.d

		hidden_effect = {
			set_capital = { state = 1051 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_completed_focus = INS_r56_secret_weapon_production
				}
				add_offsite_building = { type = arms_factory level = -2 }
			}
			clr_global_flag = INS_civil_war_ongoing
			set_global_flag = INS_civil_war_end
		}
	}

	# Disband the Militias
	option = {
		name = indonesia.415.a
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = -0.05 }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "Indonesian Militias" }
		INS_nationalize_cities_effect = yes
	}

	# Integrate the Militias
	option = {
		name = indonesia.415.b
		add_political_power = 75
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.05 }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Indonesian Militias" is_locked = no }
		INS_nationalize_cities_effect = yes
	}
}

		}

country_event = { #Commies 16 - Faction leader demanded us to reorient our party
	id = indonesia.418
	title = indonesia.418.t
	desc = indonesia.418.d

	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_sign_treaty2

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Muso Munawar leads, purge the current leader
		name = indonesia.418.a
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				PREV = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = stalinism
				}
				NOT = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = stalinism
				}
			}
		}
		retire_country_leader = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = form_majapahit_empire_flag }
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = INS_RED3
		}
		promote_character = INS_muso_munawar
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism
			popularity = -0.1
		}
		add_stability = -0.1
		PREV = {
			add_to_faction = INS
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = alliance
				id = "INS"
				value = 200
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Tan Malaka leads, purge the current leader
		name = indonesia.418.b
		trigger = {
			AND = {
				PREV = {
					OR = {
						has_country_leader_ideology = marxism
						has_country_leader_ideology = leninism
					}
				}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_leader_ideology = marxism
						has_country_leader_ideology = leninism
					}
				}
			}
		}
		retire_country_leader = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = form_majapahit_empire_flag }
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = INS_RED2
		}
		promote_character = INS_tan_malaka
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism
			popularity = -0.1
		}
		add_stability = -0.1
		PREV = {
			add_to_faction = INS
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = alliance
				id = "INS"
				value = 200
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Denounce their alliance, create our own
		name = indonesia.418.c
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = form_majapahit_empire_flag }
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = INS_RED2
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			uncomplete_national_focus = {
				focus = INS_r56_a_distant_ally
				uncomplete_children = yes
				refund_political_power = no
			}
		}
		add_war_support = 0.075
		add_political_power = 75
		complete_national_focus = INS_r56_asian_commune
		PREV = {
			add_ai_strategy = {
				type = alliance
				id = "INS"
				value = -200
			}
		}
	}
}

					name = "Amir Sjarifuddin Harahap"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			add_stability = 0.05
			else = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		promote_character = INS_amir_syarifuddin
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
				}
			}
			add_ideas = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			INS_amir_syarifuddin = {
				set_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
			}
			country_event = { id = indonesia.452 days = 1825 }
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Amir - Second term
		name = indonesia.451.f
		trigger = {

				has_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
			}
		}

		if = {

					ruling_only = yes

			}

				}

			}
			country_event = { id = indonesia.452 days = 1825 }

	option = { #Alimin

country_event = { #Commies 36 - 5th PKI National Congress - 1945-1950
	id = indonesia.452
	title = indonesia.452.t
	desc = indonesia.452.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_ins_indonesian_masses

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { #Muso - First Term
		name = indonesia.452.a
		trigger = {
			INS_muso_munawar = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
						has_character_flag = INS_second_term_flag
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Muso Munawar"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			add_stability = 0.05
			else = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = form_majapahit_empire_flag }
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = INS_RED3
		}
		promote_character = INS_muso_munawar
		add_war_support = 0.075
		add_field_marshal_role = {
			character = INS_muso_munawar
			traits = { INS_loyalty_to_indonesia politically_connected }
			skill = 1
			attack_skill = 2
			defense_skill = 1
			planning_skill = 1
			logistics_skill = 1
			legacy_id = -1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			INS_muso_munawar = {
				set_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
			}
			country_event = { id = indonesia.453 days = 1825 }
		}
	}

			}
			add_stability = 0.05
			else = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			if = {

					NOT = { has_global_flag = form_majapahit_empire_flag }
				}
				set_cosmetic_tag = INS_RED2
			}

				set_character_flag = INS_second_term_flag
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
			}
			remove_ideas = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Amir - First term
		name = indonesia.454.e
		trigger = {
			INS_amir_syarifuddin = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
						has_character_flag = INS_second_term_flag
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_leader = {
					name = "Amir Sjarifuddin Harahap"
					ruling_only = yes
				}
			}
			add_stability = 0.05
			else = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		promote_character = INS_amir_syarifuddin
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
				}
			}
			add_ideas = INS_gerindo_militias_positive
		}
		hidden_effect = {

				set_character_flag = INS_first_term_flag
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = { #Amir - Second term

		add_stability = -0.02
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = communism 
			popularity = 0.05
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #NSB 7 - Crackdown successful

	desc = indonesia.608.d

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = INS_DUTCH
			set_capital = { state = 1058 }
			clr_global_flag = INS_civil_war_ongoing
			set_global_flag = INS_civil_war_end
			DFS = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = INS
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_by = INS
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = INS_indonesian_holdouts
						is_core_of = INS
					}
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = ATJ
							is_core_of = WPG
							is_core_of = MLK
						}
					}
				}
				add_core_of = DFS
				add_resistance = 10
				start_resistance = yes
			}
		}
	}

	# Disband the Paramilitaries
	option = {
		name = indonesia.608.a
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.05 }
		delete_unit_template_and_units = { division_template = "National Socialist Brigade" }
	}

	# Integrate the Paramilitaries
	option = {
		name = indonesia.608.b
		add_political_power = 75
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = 0.05 }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "National Socialist Brigade" is_locked = no }
	}
}

				array = INS.motorized_equipment_array

				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = motorized_equipment
					amount = 100
					producer = var:weapons_country
				}
			}
			for_each_loop = {
				array = INS.support_equipment_array
				value = weapons_country
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = 75
					producer = var:weapons_country
				}
			}
			for_each_loop = {
				array = INS.artillery_equipment_array
				value = weapons_country
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment
					amount = 100
					producer = var:weapons_country
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = INS_get_trucks_flag
				}
				set_technology = { motorised_infantry = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = INS_get_artys_flag
				}
				set_technology = { gw_artillery = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = INS_get_meds_flag
				}
				set_technology = { tech_support = 1 }
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = fascism
				long_name = INS_neutrality_party_islam_long
				name = INS_neutrality_party_islam
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = INS_abikusno_cokrosuyoso
				country_leader = {
					desc = INS_abikusno_cokrosuyoso_desc
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = rexism
					traits = {
						shadow_of_cokroaminoto_f
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
				long_name = INS_pni_long
				name = INS_pni
			}
			add_country_leader_role = {
				character = INS_sukarno
				country_leader = {
					desc = INS_sukarno_desc
					expire = "1965.1.1"
					ideology = centrism
					traits = {
						overzealous_idealist
					}
				}
				promote_leader = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = INS_urip_general
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = INS_urip_general
				add_corps_commander_role = {
					character = INS_urip_sumoharjo
					traits = { INS_loyalty_to_indonesia unyielding_defender jungle_rat guerilla_fighter inflexible_strategist }
					skill = 2
					attack_skill = 2
					defense_skill = 1
					planning_skill = 1
					logistics_skill = 2
				}
				add_advisor_role = {
					character = INS_urip_sumoharjo
					advisor = {
						slot = high_command
						idea_token = INS_urip_sumoharjo
						traits = {
							army_logistics_2
						}
						cost = 50
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = INS_gatot_general
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = INS_gatot_general
				add_corps_commander_role = {
					character = INS_gatot_subroto
					traits = { INS_loyalty_to_indonesia defensive_doctrine jungle_rat }
					skill = 1
					attack_skill = 1
					defense_skill = 2

				}
				add_advisor_role = {

						ledger = army
						traits = {
							army_chief_defensive_1
						}
						cost = 50
					}
				}
			}
			if = {

					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = INS_pardi_admiral_flag
					}
				}
				add_naval_commander_role = {
					character = INS_mas_pardi
					traits = { INS_loyalty_to_indonesia bold }
					skill = 1
					attack_skill = 2
					defense_skill = 1
					maneuvering_skill = 1
					coordination_skill = 1

				}
				add_advisor_role = {

					advisor = {
						slot = navy_chief
						idea_token = INS_mas_pardi
						ledger = navy
						traits = {
							navy_chief_maneuver_1
						}
						cost = 75
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = INS_pardi_admiral_flag
			}
			if = {

					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = INS_wardiman_officer_flag
					}
				}
				add_advisor_role = {
					character = INS_wardiman
					advisor = {
						slot = air_chief
						idea_token = INS_wardiman
						ledger = air
						traits = {
							air_chief_reform_1
						}
						cost = 100
					}
				}
				INS_wardiman = {

							small = GFX_idea_indonesia_wardiman

					}
				}

country_event = { #Volksraad 180 - Event dispatcher for Thamrin's fate
	id = indonesia.220

	hidden = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = INS_thamrin_locked_up
			has_country_flag = INS_thamrin_house_arrest
		}
	}

	immediate = { #Make it 80-20 if Thamrin is locked up, but 50-50 if Thamrin is only on house arrest
		random_list = {
			80 = { #Thamrin died in prison - Historical
				INS = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.221 days = 4 random_days = 3 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					has_country_flag = INS_thamrin_house_arrest
				}
			}
			20 = { #Thamrin died in his house
				INS = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.222 days = 6 random_days = 4 }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					has_country_flag = INS_thamrin_locked_up
				}
			}
			20 = { #Thamrin survives
				INS = {
					country_event = { id = indonesia.223 days = 6 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		name = indonesia.259.b
		INS = {
			HOL_jacques_de_kadt = {
				set_character_flag = INS_de_kadt_accepted
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = available_political_advisor
		show_ideas_tooltip = HOL_jacques_de_kadt
		add_stability = -0.02
		add_political_power = -10
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Aging 1 - Syahrir
	id = indonesia.901
	hidden = yes

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		OR = {
			date > 1944.03.05
			AND = {
				date > 1939.03.05
				OR = {
					has_completed_focus = INS_expel_the_colonials
					has_completed_focus = INS_merdeka
				}
			}
		}
		INS_sutan_syahrir = {
			has_trait = silent_mule
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 2 }

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = INS_sutan_syahrir
			}
			set_country_flag = INS_syahrir_stays
		}
		INS_sutan_syahrir = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_country_leader_traits = {
					remove = silent_mule
					add = silent_workhorse
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				add_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = silent_workhorse
				}
				remove_trait = {
					slot = political_advisor
					trait = silent_mule
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = INS_syahrir_stays
			}
			activate_advisor = INS_sutan_syahrir
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Aging 8 - Jan Hendrik Wesseling
	id = indonesia.908
	title = indonesia.908.t
	desc = indonesia.908.desc

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		date > 1939.01.01
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		has_character = INS_wesseling
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }

	option = { #if not at war, he can be retired from field duty
		name = indonesia.908.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retiring_age_flag
			}
		}
		INS_wesseling = {
			remove_unit_leader_role = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retiring_age_flag
			set_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retired_flag
			country_event = {
				id = indonesia.908
				days = 1826
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #if at war, we can extend his service with a bit of cost; if not, extending it would cost a few more
		name = indonesia.908.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = INS_wesseling_extended_flag
					has_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retiring_age_flag
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_war = no
				}
				has_political_power > 14
				command_power > 19
				has_army_experience > 19
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = no
			}
			add_political_power = -15
			add_command_power = -20
			army_experience = -20
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = yes
			}
			add_political_power = -5
			add_command_power = -10
			army_experience = -5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retiring_age_flag
			set_country_flag = INS_wesseling_extended_flag
			country_event = {
				id = indonesia.908
				days = 1826
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #War or not, if he's 60, he's retired. But he can be also retired early
		name = indonesia.908.c
		retire_character = INS_wesseling
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = INS_wesseling_retiring_age_flag
				}
			}
			add_command_power = 5
			army_experience = 10
		}
	}
}

			add_command_power = -20
			army_experience = -20
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = yes
			}
			add_political_power = -5
			add_command_power = -10
			army_experience = -5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_van_tricht_retiring_age_flag
			set_country_flag = INS_van_tricht_extended_flag
			country_event = {
				id = indonesia.912
				days = 1826
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #War or not, if he's 60, he's retired. But he can be also retired early
		name = indonesia.912.c
		retire_character = INS_aleid_van_tricht
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = INS_van_tricht_retiring_age_flag
				}
			}
			add_command_power = 5
			army_experience = 10
		}
	}
}

	id = indonesia.920
	title = indonesia.920.t
	desc = indonesia.920.d

	is_triggered_only = no
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		date > 1947.7.14
		OR = {
			has_completed_focus = INS_local_militias
			has_completed_focus = INS_strict_hierarchy
		}
		OR = {
			has_completed_focus = INS_flexible_navy
			has_completed_focus = INS_dominant_navy
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = indonesia.920.a
		add_naval_commander_role = {
			character = INS_subijakto
			traits = { fleet_protector }
			skill = 1
			attack_skill = 1
			defense_skill = 2
			maneuvering_skill = 1
			coordination_skill = 1
			legacy_id = -1
		}
		add_advisor_role = {
			character = INS_subijakto
			advisor = {
				slot = navy_chief
				idea_token = INS_subijakto
				ledger = navy
				traits = {
					navy_chief_decisive_battle_1
				}
				cost = 75
			}
		}
	}
}

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = INS
		date > 1936.03.01
		has_start_date < 1936.01.02
		has_character = INS_maarten_van_dulm
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 1 }

	option = { #if not at war, he can be retired from field duty
		name = indonesia.919.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_retiring_age_flag
			}
		}
		INS_maarten_van_dulm = {
			remove_unit_leader_role = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_retiring_age_flag
			country_event = {
				id = indonesia.923
				days = 1251
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #if at war, we can extend his service with a bit of cost; if not, extending it would cost a few more
		name = indonesia.923.b
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_extended_flag
					has_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_retiring_age_flag
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_war = no
				}
				has_political_power > 14
				command_power > 19
				has_navy_experience > 19
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_war = yes
				}
				has_political_power > 4
				command_power > 9
				has_navy_experience > 4
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = no
			}
			add_political_power = -15
			add_command_power = -20
			navy_experience = -20
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war = yes
			}
			add_political_power = -5
			add_command_power = -10
			navy_experience = -5
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_retiring_age_flag
			set_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_extended_flag
			country_event = {
				id = indonesia.923
				days = 1251
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #War or not, if he's 60, he's retired. But he can be also retired early
		name = indonesia.923.c
		retire_character = INS_maarten_van_dulm
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = INS_maarten_van_dulm_retiring_age_flag
				}
			}
			add_command_power = 5
			navy_experience = 10
		}
	}
}
